    [
      "age-related or service-year/experience-based pay steps for Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: A salary scale consists of a maximum of 11 periodic steps, indicated by the numbers 0 to 10. Annually, a regular periodic increase is granted on January 1st, provided the employee has been employed for at least six months. In case of insufficient performance, a periodic increase may not be granted, with written notification before January 1st. If significant improvement in performance occurs, the employer may still grant a periodic increase. Periodic increases can be granted until the maximum of the function scale is reached."
    ],
    [
      "rules on entry placement in the wage scale for Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: Upon appointment, the employee receives the salary corresponding to periodic step 0 of the function scale. In cases of relevant experience with another employer (at the employer's discretion), the salary may be set at a higher periodic step, but not exceeding the maximum of the function scale. If an employee does not yet possess the required knowledge and skills for full job fulfillment upon appointment, placement in a 'aanloopschaal' (entry scale) may occur. The entry scale is one salary scale lower than the function scale. For function level 1, placement always occurs in the function scale. Placement in the entry scale is generally for a maximum of one year for function groups 1 to 6, and a maximum of two years for groups 7 to 12."
    ],
    [
      "rules on personal allowances when the maximum of a wage scale is reached or when an employee keeps a higher wage after reclassification for Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: If a new reference function is classified lower than the existing function, the employee is reclassified to the correct function scale at the next salary round, choosing the step closest to, but lower than, the existing salary. The difference between the existing gross salary and the new gross scale salary is granted as a personal gross allowance (PT). The new lower gross scale salary is included in CAO increases, while the PT is reduced by these increases, maintaining the gross monthly salary at the original level until it matches the new scale salary. If the employee has not yet reached the end of the new function scale, they are eligible for an extra step per year of experience, which accelerates the reduction of the PT. The employer may deviate positively, for example, by applying CAO increases to the entire salary, in which case the personal gross allowance increases with the CAO increases."
    ],

      "general wage increases for Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: The salary increases are also applicable to on-call allowances."

      "bonuses and incentive schemes for Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: - Service anniversary bonus: One gross monthly salary for 25 years of uninterrupted service, and one gross monthly salary for 40 years of uninterrupted service. The amount is paid net to the extent permitted by tax law. For participants in the senior scheme, the 100% salary is used instead of 90% (full-time basis). - Holiday allowance: 8% of the gross annual salary. - First Aid and/or Emergency Response Team allowance: A net allowance of € 5.00 per month for employees who, at the employer's request, hold a valid First Aid and/or Emergency Response Team diploma. - On-call allowance: For employees on call, a gross compensation per service is paid: € 10.38 for weekdays (7 PM - 7 AM), € 15.57 for Saturdays (12 AM - 7 PM), and € 20.76 for Sundays and public holidays (12 AM - 12 AM). As of January 1, 2019, these amounts are € 10.54, € 15.80, and € 21.07 respectively. These amounts are increased with CAO wage increases."

    [
      "job classifications, function groups, pay groups/grades for Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: The salary structure is based on job classifications in salary scales (1-12) and periodic steps. The classification is based on job profiles valued according to the USB (Universal Berenschot System) job evaluation system. The job profiles with their classification in the salary table are set out in the job matrix."
    ],
    [
      "age-related or service-year/experience-based pay steps for Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Each salary scale consists of a number of periodic steps, with periodic step 0 being the starting salary. Annual periodic increases are granted on January 1st, provided the employee has received a sufficient performance review. An insufficient review means no periodic increase. A sufficient to good review means one periodic increase. An excellent review means two periodic increases, subject to prior approval from the management. If no review has taken place through no fault of the employee, a periodic increase is granted. If the manager has been in their role for less than six months during the review period, a periodic increase is also granted. When a general pay round occurs, the salary table is adjusted first, then the periodic increases are granted. Employees who have worked for less than six months at the time of the normal review round will receive a periodic increase as of July 1st, based on a sufficient interim review. Employees who missed the normal review round due to long-term disability or leave, but worked for at least six months in the review year and resumed work before July 1st, may receive a periodic increase from the first day of the month following a sufficient interim review."
    ],
    [
      "rules on entry placement in the wage scale for Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: If the employee, upon appointment (both internal and external applications), does not yet possess the knowledge and skills required for full job fulfillment, placement in an entry scale ('aanloopschaal') occurs. The entry scale is one salary scale lower than the function scale. For salary scale 1, placement always occurs in the function scale. Placement in the entry scale is for a minimum of six months. If the employee masters the function well in all aspects, transfer to the function scale occurs, observing promotion rules. If the employee cannot be transferred to the function scale after two years, agreements will be made about a follow-up trajectory with possible legal consequences (transfer, dismissal). The moment of transfer from the entry scale to the function scale is considered the formal moment of promotion. If the employee, upon appointment, actually possesses the knowledge and skills required for full job fulfillment, direct placement in the function scale occurs. In the case of an external application, the salary may be set at a higher periodic step than periodic step 0 if, in the opinion of the manager, there is relevant experience with another employer. The maximum of the corresponding salary scale cannot be exceeded. For internal applications, the moment of placement in the function scale is considered the formal moment of promotion."
    ],
    [
      "rules on personal allowances when the maximum of a wage scale is reached or when an employee keeps a higher wage after reclassification for Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: - Placement at a lower function level: Upon (re)placement at a lower function level, the employee is transferred to the next higher salary in the lower salary scale. If the employee already has a salary higher than the maximum salary in the lower salary scale, a phase-out scheme applies, gradually reducing the old salary over a three-year period until the maximum salary in the lower salary scale is reached. Any 'perspectiefgarantie' (perspective guarantee) from previous salary table adjustments (right to 'uitloop-periodieken' in the old salary table) lapses upon placement at a lower function level and is replaced by a new perspective from the 'uitloop-periodieken' in the new (lower) scale. In specific situations (medical indication or reorganization), the redeployment policy framework is leading, and the phase-out scheme does not apply. - Acting allowance: If an employee, at the request of the manager, fully or partially performs a function classified in a higher salary scale for a continuous period of at least two months, a temporary acting allowance is granted for the entire period. The allowance is 5% of the employee's own function salary, applies to acting for more than 50% of working hours, and is calculated on the full salary. It is adjusted with general salary scale increases. - Labor market allowance: The management may grant a labor market allowance for a maximum of three years to new and existing employees if the labor market situation necessitates it. The allowance is a fixed gross amount, not related to scale or periodic step, and requires prior management approval. It is adjusted with general salary scale increases. - Performance allowance: In exceptional cases, a temporary allowance may be granted to employees who have reached the maximum of their salary scale if their personal qualities lead to job fulfillment exceeding the job profile in level and content. This allowance is a fixed gross amount, not related to scale or periodic step, and requires prior management approval. Its continuation is reviewed annually during the performance review round. All these allowances are granted gross, paid monthly with salary, and count towards holiday pay and pensionable earnings."
    ],
    [
      "bonuses and incentive schemes for Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: - Holiday allowance: 8.25% of the gross annual salary. - On-call allowance: For employees on call, a gross compensation per service is paid: € 10.75 for weekdays (7 PM - 7 AM), € 16.12 for Saturdays (12 AM - 12 AM), and € 21.49 for Sundays and public holidays (12 AM - 12 AM). These amounts are increased with CAO wage increases. Hours worked during an on-call service are paid according to the inconvenience scheme, and the on-call allowance for these hours is forfeited. Compensation for hours worked is 1:1 in time."
    ],
    [
      "notes explaining how the wage system or tables operate for Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: The amounts stated in the salary table are gross monthly salaries based on a 37-hour work week. For a working week of less than 37 hours, the pro-rata principle applies. In a general pay round, the amounts within the scale are adjusted. Employees covered by the Participation Act are entitled to a salary of 120% of the statutory minimum (youth) wage. Existing agreements prior to March 1, 2019, remain in effect. The 1% wage increase as of January 1, 2019, applies to all periodic steps, including 'uitloopperiodieken' (outflow periodic steps). Other agreed wage increases do not apply to these 'uitloopperiodieken', which are frozen as long as they exceed the scale maximum of the function scale. Once they are caught up, they will again be subject to CAO wage increases."

    [
      "age-related or service-year/experience-based pay steps for Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Salary increases based on 'functiejaren' (function years) occur on January 1st of each calendar year, unless the employee has not yet reached the age of 21 or was not employed on January 1st of the preceding calendar year. These increases are not automatic but depend on sufficient performance, documented by a written performance review. If an increase is not granted, it must be granted within one year. Employees aged 20 and younger receive the hourly wage corresponding to their age within their function group. When an employee's age changes, their hourly wage is increased from the payment date following the age change. An employee turning 21 receives the scale hourly wage in year class 0 of their function group from the payment period following their 21st birthday."
    ],
    [
      "rules on entry placement in the wage scale for Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: An employee placed in a new function will be initially classified one level lower in the salary structure than normal for that function. With sufficient performance, they will be classified at the appropriate salary level for the function after a maximum of one year. Any performance deficiencies preventing this must be communicated in writing and with reasons before the end of that year. Vacation workers receive the minimum wage for the first 3 months, and then 120% of the minimum wage."

    [
      "rules on personal allowances when the maximum of a wage scale is reached or when an employee keeps a higher wage after reclassification for Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: - Acting allowance: An employee who, at the explicit instruction of the employer, fully or partially performs a function classified in a higher salary scale for a continuous period of at least two months, receives a temporary acting allowance for the entire period. The allowance is the difference between the current salary and the starting salary (of the function scale) of the function being acted in, with a minimum of two periodic steps. If the acting period exceeds two months, the employer must make a definitive decision regarding a higher function classification for the substitute. - Reclassification to a lower function: If an employee is transferred to a lower-classified function, whether at their own request or due to circumstances beyond their control, the salary adjustment takes effect from the payment period following the transfer. If the transfer is at the employee's own request and they are 21 or older, the salary reduction is half the difference between the 0-function-year salaries of the two scales, or more if needed to match the next lower amount in the lower scale. If the transfer is due to circumstances beyond the employee's control and they are 21 or older, they receive an hourly wage at least equal to what they earned before the transfer."

  "leave_information": [
    [
      "maternity leave for Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: In case of pregnancy, there is generally an entitlement to a benefit under the Work and Care Act for 16 weeks, which Natuurmonumenten supplements if necessary to the level of the last earned nominal salary, excluding allowances. The benefit starts six weeks before the expected delivery date. It is possible to work longer before delivery, but not longer than four weeks before the expected delivery date. The period thus saved extends the benefit period after delivery. However, the employee always has the right to at least ten weeks of leave after delivery. If, for example, the employee stopped working six weeks in advance and the baby is two weeks late, only eight weeks (out of sixteen) would remain after delivery. The two weeks the baby is late are added to the remaining leave weeks, making the total leave period eighteen weeks. If the baby is born prematurely, the total leave period remains 16 weeks. The employee can take the last period of maternity leave in parts. This applies to leave remaining after six weeks from the date of delivery. This part of the leave can be taken flexibly in consultation with the manager over a maximum period of 30 weeks. The total duration of the leave does not change as a result."

With the introduction of the 'Wet invoering extra geboorteverlof' (WIEG) as of July 1, 2020, partners can take up to 5 working weeks of additional paternity leave. They will receive a benefit of 70% of their daily wage, with a maximum of 70% of the maximum daily wage. UWV pays for these weeks of leave. The employee must take these leave weeks within 6 months after the child's birth. A prerequisite is that an employee first takes the paternity leave of once the weekly working hours. An employee must also apply for leave in full weeks. In consultation with Natuurmonumenten, the employee can spread the additional leave over a period longer than 5 weeks. It is also possible to take less than 5 weeks of additional paternity leave. If Natuurmonumenten has a compelling business or service interest, it may, in consultation with the employee, change the spread of the leave."

Each parent is entitled to a maximum of six weeks of adoption leave. The right to this leave starts two weeks before the child is taken into adoption or foster care and can be taken up to 16 weeks thereafter. The date of placement in the family must be evidenced by a document submitted to HR. If two or more children are adopted simultaneously, the right to leave applies only to one child."

For employees with less than 35% disability, if they are deemed less than 35% disabled by UWV after the first year and remain employed by the same employer, 90% of the salary is paid for a maximum of 5 years."

    [
      "job classifications, function groups, pay groups/grades and how they link to wage scales:",
      "Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: The salary table contains 11 salary scales (2 to 12), each with a maximum of 11 increments (0 to 10). These scales correspond to the function level as defined in a function profile. A 'Functieniveaumatrix Landschappen' (Appendix 2) provides a framework for classifying job families (management and leadership, programs and projects, advisory, specialists/experts, administration, execution, support) and reference functions into these scales.",
      "Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: The salary table contains 12 salary scales (1 to 12), each with a number of increments. Functions are classified into salary scales based on function profiles valued according to the USB (Universal System Berenschot) function valuation system. The function profiles and their classification are set out in the function matrix.",
      "Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Functions are classified into function groups (I-VII) and linked to a system of function-based remuneration. The 'Functiematrix' (Appendix I) outlines these classifications and descriptions for each function group."
    ],
    [
      "age-related or service-year/experience-based pay steps (trede/periodieken):",
      "Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: A salary scale consists of a maximum of 11 increments (periodieken), indicated by the numbers 0 to 10. Annually, a regular periodic increase is granted as of January 1st, provided the employee has been employed for at least six months and performance is sufficient. Periodic increases can be granted until the maximum of the function scale is reached.",
      "Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Each salary scale consists of a number of increments (periodieken), with increment 0 as the starting salary. Annually, periodic increases are granted as of January 1st of each year, provided performance is sufficient. Periodic increases can be granted until the maximum of the salary scale is reached.",
      "Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: The time wage is determined by the function group and the employee's age. Employees aged 20 and younger receive the time wage corresponding to their age within their function group. Upon reaching 21 years of age, the employee receives the scale time wage in year class 0 of their function group. Increases based on function years are not automatic but depend on sufficient performance."
    ],
    [
      "rules on entry placement in the wage scale:",
      "Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: The employee starts at increment 0 of the function scale. With relevant experience from another employer, the salary can be set at a higher increment, but not above the scale maximum. If the employee lacks full function fulfillment skills, classification in a 'aanloopschaal' (starting scale) is possible, which is one scale lower than the function scale. This typically lasts up to one year for function groups 1-6, and up to two years for groups 7-12. For function level 1, direct placement in the function scale applies.",
      "Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Direct classification in the function scale occurs if the employee possesses all required knowledge and skills. For external hires, the salary can be set at a higher increment than 0 based on relevant experience, but not exceeding the scale maximum. If skills are not fully met, classification in a 'aanloopschaal' (one scale lower) is possible for a minimum of six months. For salary scale 1, direct placement in the function scale applies.",
      "Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: An employee placed in a new function will initially be classified one level lower than normal for that function. With sufficient performance, they will be moved to the correct salary level after a maximum of one year."
    ],
    [
      "rules on personal allowances when the maximum of a wage scale is reached or when an employee keeps a higher wage after reclassification:",
      "Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: If a new reference function is classified lower than the existing one, the employee is placed in the correct, lower function scale at the closest lower increment. The difference between the old and new gross salary is granted as a personal gross allowance (PT). This PT is reduced by future CAO wage increases, maintaining the original gross monthly salary until it matches the new scale salary. If the employee is not at the end of the new scale, they still receive annual increments, which accelerate the reduction of the PT. Employers may deviate positively, e.g., by applying CAO increases to the entire salary, allowing the PT to grow with CAO increases.",
      "Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Employees with a 'garantietoeslag' (guarantee allowance from 2003) are transferred to a new salary scale at their current salary level (scale salary + guarantee allowance + promotion increment). If this exceeds the new scale maximum, the old guarantee allowance lapses, and any excess is converted into a new guarantee allowance. For placement at a lower function level, the employee is transferred to the next higher salary in the lower scale. If the current salary is above the new scale maximum, a three-year phase-out scheme applies. A 'perspective guarantee' (right to 'uitloopperiodieken' from 2005) lapses upon lower placement and is replaced by a new perspective within the lower scale's 'uitloopperiodieken'. In specific cases (medical indication, reorganization), a separate redeployment policy applies instead of the phase-out scheme."
    ],

      "Employees in the entry-level scale (aanloopschaal) are assessed; with a 'sufficient' rating, they remain in the entry-level scale; with a 'good' rating, they are promoted to the regular scale. New classifications/periodic increases take effect on January 1st and July 1st.",

      "Rules on entry placement in the wage scale (Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen):",
      "Upon appointment, the employee receives the salary corresponding to increment 0 of the function scale.",
      "In case of relevant experience with another employer (in the employer's judgment), the salary may be set at a higher increment, but not higher than the maximum of the function scale.",
      "If the employee does not yet possess the required knowledge and skills for full function fulfillment upon appointment, placement in an entry-level scale (aanloopschaal) may occur.     [
      "Rules on personal allowances when the maximum of a wage scale is reached or when an employee keeps a higher wage after reclassification (Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen):",
      "If the new reference function is classified lower than the existing function, the employee is reclassified to the correct function scale at the next salary round, choosing the increment closest to, but lower than, the existing salary. The difference between the existing gross salary and the new gross scale salary is granted as a personal gross allowance (PT). The new lower gross scale salary is included in CAO increases. The PT is reduced by the CAO increases, maintaining the gross monthly salary at the original level until it matches the new scale salary. If the employee has not yet reached the maximum of the new function scale, they are eligible for an extra increment per year of experience, which accelerates the reduction of the PT. The employer may deviate positively, e.g., by applying CAO increases to the entire salary, in which case the personal gross allowance increases with CAO increases. These rules are part of the CAO; only positive deviations for the employee are allowed, and chosen rules must be known and communicated in advance."

      "Job classifications, function groups, pay groups/grades and how they link to wage scales (Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten): Functions are classified into salary scales based on function profiles valued according to the USB (Universeel Systeem Berenschot) function valuation system. The function profiles and their classification in the salary table are set out in the function matrix."
    ],
    [
      "Age-related or service-year/experience-based pay steps (Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten): Each salary scale consists of a number of increments, with increment 0 being the starting salary. Annual increments are awarded on January 1st each year, provided the employee has a 'sufficient' performance rating."
    ],
    [
      "Rules governing progression within scales (Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten):",
      "A 'sufficient' performance rating is a prerequisite for awarding the annual increment. Annual increments are awarded on January 1st each year until the maximum of the salary scale is reached.",
      "With an 'insufficient' rating, no increment is awarded on January 1st.",
      "With a 'sufficient' to 'good' rating, one increment is awarded.",
      "With an 'excellent' rating, two increments are awarded, subject to prior approval from management.",
      "If no assessment has taken place through no fault of the employee, while the employee would have been eligible, the annual increment is awarded.",
      "Employees who have worked for less than six months at the time of the regular assessment round will still receive an increment on July 1st based on a 'sufficient' interim assessment.",
      "Promotion occurs when an employee is placed in the function scale of a function classified in a higher salary scale. Promotion requires a 'sufficient' performance rating, indicating the function is performed adequately and fully. Promotions generally take place on January 1st.",
      "For promotions, the new salary is the next higher amount in the new salary scale. If the difference between the old and new salary is less than €10, an extra increment is awarded.",
      "If a promotion coincides with the annual periodic increase, a regular increment is awarded in the old salary scale if possible. Subsequently, the employee moves to the next higher amount in the new salary scale. No increments are given if this leads to a salary above the maximum of the new salary scale."
    ],
    [
      "Rules on entry placement in the wage scale (Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten):",
      "If, upon appointment (internal or external), the employee does not yet possess the knowledge and skills required for full function fulfillment, placement in an entry-level scale (aanloopschaal) occurs. The entry-level scale is one salary scale lower than the function scale. For salary scale 1, placement always occurs in the function scale. Placement in the entry-level scale is for a minimum of six months. If the employee masters the function well in all aspects, transfer to the function scale occurs according to promotion rules. If the employee cannot be transferred to the function scale after two years, agreements will be made about a follow-up trajectory with possible legal consequences (transfer, dismissal).",
      "For external applications, the salary may be set at a higher increment than increment 0 if, in the opinion of the manager, there is relevant experience with another employer. The maximum of the corresponding salary scale cannot be exceeded."
    ],
    [
      "Rules on personal allowances when the maximum of a wage scale is reached or when an employee keeps a higher wage after reclassification (Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten):",
      "Placement at a lower function level: Upon (re)placement at a lower function level, the employee is transferred to the next higher salary in the lower salary scale. If the employee already has a salary higher than the maximum salary in the lower salary scale, a phase-out scheme applies, gradually reducing the old salary over a three-year period until the maximum salary in the lower salary scale is reached. If the employee has a 'perspective guarantee' from the 2005 salary table adjustments (right to out-of-scale increments in the 'old' salary table), this is forfeited upon placement at a lower function level and replaced by a new perspective from the out-of-scale increments in the new (lower) scale. In certain situations (medical indication or reorganization), the 'reassignment policy framework' applies, overriding the phase-out scheme.",
      "Acting Allowance: If an employee, at the request of the manager, fully or partially performs a function classified in a higher salary scale for a minimum of two consecutive months, a temporary acting allowance is granted for the entire period. The allowance is 5% of the employee's own function salary, applicable for acting for more than 50% of working hours, calculated on the full salary. It is adjusted with general salary scale increases.",
      "Labor Market Allowance: Management may, if labor market conditions require it, grant a labor market allowance to new and existing employees for a maximum of three years. The allowance is a fixed gross amount, not related to scale or increment, and is adjusted with general salary scale increases. Manager HR must be consulted, and management approval is required.",
      "Performance Allowance: In exceptional cases, a temporary allowance may be granted to employees who have reached the maximum of their salary scale if their personal qualities lead to function fulfillment exceeding the function profile in level and content. The allowance is a fixed gross amount, not related to scale or increment, and is adjusted with general salary scale increases. Manager HR must be consulted, and management approval is required. Annual review determines if the allowance continues."

      "Bonuses and incentive schemes beyond base pay (Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten):",
      "Inconvenience Allowance: The employee receives an allowance on the gross hourly wage according to the nominal salary (excluding any phase-out scheme amount) for the actually worked hours: 25% for weekdays from 19:00 to 07:00; 25% for Saturdays from 00:00 to 19:00; 50% for Saturdays from 19:00 to 24:00; 50% for Sundays and public holidays from 00:00 to 24:00. Work performed on mandatory leave days set by management is equated with work on Saturdays."

The year for which the holiday allowance is calculated runs from June 1st to May 31st. The holiday allowance is paid retrospectively with the salary payment for May. Upon termination of employment, any outstanding holiday allowance is paid. If the employment contract has not lasted a full year, the holiday allowance is paid proportionally."

      "Personal Gross Allowance (PT) for Lower Reclassification (Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen)",
      "If a new reference function is classified lower than the existing function, the employee will be classified in the correct function scale at the next salary round, choosing the increment closest to the existing salary, albeit lower. The difference between the existing gross salary and the new gross scale salary will be granted as a personal gross allowance (PT). The new lower gross scale salary will be included in the CLA increases. The PT will be reduced by the CLA increases. In this way, the gross monthly salary remains equal to the original salary until it reaches the level of the new scale salary. If the employee has not yet reached the end of the new function scale, the employee will still be eligible for an extra increment per year of experience, in accordance with the CLA. This will reduce the PT faster. The employer may deviate from this in a positive sense for the employee (e.g., by applying CLA increases to the entire salary, in which case the personal gross allowance grows with CLA increases). Deviations are only allowed in a positive sense for the employee. The chosen rules must be known in advance and communicated to the employees."

      "Commuting Allowance - Public Transport (Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen)",
      "Travel by public transport is reimbursed based on 2nd class. The actual costs incurred are reimbursed. Annual and monthly passes are reimbursed as an advance based on proof from the carrier. The proof is submitted to the personnel administration after the validity period. The cheapest form of transport is reimbursed. For employees who choose a more expensive form of transport, the additional costs are for their own account. Depending on the number of travel days per month, this is either a monthly (or annual) route pass. If an employee has purchased a discount card and uses it for business purposes, they may declare the full fare of the train tickets. The full fare may be declared until the employee has received the benefit equal to the purchase costs. From that moment on, the employee declares - for the remaining term of the discount card - only at the discount rate. A copy of the discount card is submitted with the first declaration. This method of declaration applies to both commuting and business travel."

If significant improvement in performance occurs, the employer may still award a periodic increment.", "Periodic increases can be awarded until the maximum of the function scale is reached."], ["Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: Personal Allowance (Persoonlijke Toeslag - PT) at lower reclassification", "When reclassified to a lower function scale, the difference between the existing gross salary and the new gross scale salary is awarded as a personal gross allowance (PT). The new lower gross scale salary is included in the CAO increases. The PT is reduced by the CAO increases. In this way, the gross monthly salary remains equal to the original salary until it reaches the level of the new scale salary. If the employee has not yet reached the end of the new function scale, the employee is eligible for an extra periodic increment per year of experience, in accordance with the CAO. This will reduce the PT faster.", "The employer may deviate from this in a positive sense for the employee, e.g., by applying the CAO increases to the entire salary. The personal gross allowance grows with the CAO increases."], ["Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: Acting Allowance", "The employee who, at the instruction of the employer or their representative, fully performs a function classified in a higher salary scale than their own for a minimum of two consecutive months, is eligible for an acting allowance.", "The amount of the acting allowance is the difference in salary between the employee's own function and the temporarily higher function, measured based on the 0-periodic increments."], ["Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: Service Jubilee Gratuity", "The employee receives for an uninterrupted employment of:", "25 years: one gross monthly salary", "40 years: one gross monthly salary", "The amount is paid net insofar as fiscally permitted. After that, the compensation expires. If overtime is performed in the last quarter of the calendar year, the compensation time may be taken up to and including March 31 of the following calendar year.", "Overtime compensation is taken first when taking holiday hours.", "Overtime hours are rounded to full quarters. Compensation can be taken per hour.", "Overtime hours must be taken within four weeks. If the employer indicates that this is not possible, the following extra compensation in time applies to employees classified in salary scales 1 to 6:", "working days between 19:00 – 7:00: 25%", "Saturday from 00:00 to 19:00: 25%", "Saturday from 19:00 to 24:00: 50%", "Sunday and public holidays from 00:00 to 24:00: 50%"], ["Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: EHBO and/or BHV Allowance", "An employee who, at the employer's instruction, holds a valid First Aid (EHBO) and/or Emergency Response (BHV) diploma receives a net allowance of €5.00 per month."], ["Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: Company Clothing Allowance", "Employees who are required to wear uniform and work clothing will receive a monthly net allowance of €12.00 as a contribution to the costs for cleaning and maintenance of the clothing."], ["Ondernemingsdeel De Landschappen: Travel Allowances", "Public Transport: Travel by public transport is reimbursed based on 2nd class. The actual costs incurred are reimbursed. Annual and monthly tickets are reimbursed as an advance based on proof from the carrier. The cheapest form of transport is reimbursed. For employees who choose a more expensive form of transport, the extra costs are for their own account. If an employee has purchased a discount card and uses it for business, they may declare the full fare of the train tickets until they have received the benefit equal to the purchase costs. The salary continuation obligation during the second period of 26 weeks (within the first year of disability) amounts to the statutory salary continuation of 70%, plus a supplement of 20%.", "3. The salary is paid, after deduction of legally mandatory and permitted amounts, on or before the end of each month by transfer to the employee's IBAN number.", "If facilities for work are made available to the employee, Natuurmonumenten is entitled to deduct any personal contribution from the salary."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Holiday Allowance", "The employee receives a holiday allowance of 8.25% of the gross annual salary.", "The year over which the holiday allowance is calculated, runs from June 1 to May 31.", "The holiday allowance is paid retrospectively with the salary payment for the month of May.", "Upon termination of the employment contract, payment of any outstanding holiday allowance takes place.", "If the employment contract has not lasted a full year, the holiday allowance will be paid proportionally."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Wage Scale for BBL Students and Participation Act Employees", "The wage scale for BBL students and for employees working under the Participation Act is the statutory minimum (youth) wage plus 20%. Agreements existing before March 1, 2019, remain in effect."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Wage Increases and Outflow Periodic Increments", "The wage increase of 1% as of January 1, 2019, applies to all periodic increments at Natuurmonumenten, including the 'uitloopperiodieken' (outflow periodic increments). The other agreed wage increases are NOT applicable to these 'uitloopperiodieken'. The 'uitloopperiodieken' are 'frozen'. This means that the 'uitloopperiodieken' after January 1, 2019, are no longer eligible for CAO wage increases, as long as these periodic increments exceed the scale maximum of the function scale. If these have been caught up, they will, of course, again participate in the CAO wage increases."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Job Classification and Progression", "Job Classification: The salary structure is based on the classification of functions into salary scales. The classification takes place based on job profiles valued according to the Berenschot Universal System (USB) job evaluation system. The job profiles with their classification in the salary table are set out in the function matrix.", "Entry-level scale: If the employee, upon appointment, does not yet possess the knowledge and skills required for full job performance, placement in the entry-level scale occurs. The entry-level scale is one salary scale lower than the function scale. For salary scale 1, placement always occurs in the function scale. Placement in the entry-level scale takes place for a minimum of six months. If the employee fully masters the function in all aspects, transfer to the function scale takes place, observing the rules for promotion. If the employee cannot be transferred to the function scale after two years, agreements will be made about a follow-up trajectory with possible legal consequences (transfer, dismissal). The moment of transfer from the entry-level scale to the function scale is considered the formal moment of promotion.", "Function scale: If the employee, upon appointment, actually possesses the knowledge and skills required for full job performance, direct placement in the function scale occurs. For an external application, the salary can be set at a higher periodic increment than periodic increment 0 if - in the opinion of the manager - there is relevant experience with another employer. The maximum of the corresponding salary scale cannot, however, be exceeded. For internal applications, the moment of placement in the function scale is considered the formal moment of promotion.", "Annual periodic increments: A prerequisite for awarding the annual periodic increment is a sufficient assessment. The annual periodic increments are awarded on January 1 of each year. Annual periodic increments can be awarded until the maximum in the salary scale is reached: For an insufficient assessment, no periodic increment is awarded on January 1; For a sufficient to good assessment, one periodic increment is awarded; For an excellent assessment, two periodic increments are awarded. This award can only take place after prior approval from the management.", "Promotions: Promotion occurs when the employee is placed in the function scale of a function classified in a higher salary scale. A promotion can only take place after a sufficient assessment, with the outcome that the function is performed sufficiently and fully. In principle, all promotions take place on January 1. The new salary is the next higher amount in the new salary scale. In the old salary scale, one promotion periodic increment (plus an annual periodic increment if the promotion takes place on January 1) is awarded. If the maximum of the old salary scale has already been reached, the promotion periodic increment (plus the annual periodic increment) is awarded according to the steps in the old salary scale, as if there were still periodic increments to follow in the scale. After that, classification in the new salary scale takes place at the next higher amount. No periodic increments are given if this leads to a salary that exceeds the maximum of the new salary scale.", "Placement at a lower function level: For (re)placement at a lower function level, the employee is transferred to the next higher salary in the lower salary scale. If the employee already has a salary that is higher than the maximum salary in the lower salary scale, a phase-out scheme applies, whereby the old salary is gradually reduced over a period of three years until the maximum salary in the lower salary scale is reached. If the employee has a 'perspectiefgarantie' (perspective guarantee) resulting from the agreements regarding the salary table adjusted in 2005 (right to outflow periodic increments in the 'old' salary table), this lapses upon placement at a lower function level. In its place comes a new perspective from the outflow periodic increments in the new (in this case, lower) scale."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Allowances", "Acting Allowance: If the employee, at the request of the manager, fully or partially performs a function classified in a higher salary scale than their own for a minimum of two consecutive months, a temporary acting allowance is awarded for the entire period of acting. The amount of the acting allowance is set at 5% of the salary in the employee's own function. The allowance applies to acting for more than 50% of the working time and is calculated over the full salary. With a general increase in salary scales, the acting allowance is adjusted accordingly.", "Labor Market Allowance: The management can - if the situation on the labor market makes this necessary - award a labor market allowance to new employees and existing employees for a maximum period of three years. After that, it will be reviewed whether the allowance is still justified. The labor market allowance cannot be related to a scale or periodic increment but is set at a specific gross amount. With a general increase in salary scales, the labor market allowance is adjusted accordingly.", "Performance Allowance: In exceptional cases, it is possible to award a temporary allowance to employees who have reached the maximum of their salary scale. The criterion is that due to the employee's personal qualities, the job performance in terms of level and content exceeds the job profile. The performance allowance cannot be related to a scale or periodic increment but is set at a specific gross amount. With a general increase in salary scales, the performance allowance is adjusted accordingly. Annually, during the assessment round, it is determined whether the allowance is still justified."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Overtime Compensation", "The employee receives compensation for overtime in the form of free time, equal to the number of hours the overtime lasted.", "For overtime during unusual hours, the employee receives a time-for-time supplement per hour worked, namely:", "on working days from 19:00 to 07:00: 25% extra time-for-time", "on Saturdays from 00:00 to 19:00: 25% extra time-for-time", "on Saturdays from 19:00 to 24:00: 50% extra time-for-time", "on Sundays and public holidays from 00:00 to 24:00: 50% extra time-for-time", "Note: this time supplement is not applicable if it concerns shifted hours!", "Compensation hours must be taken within three months after the end of the quarter in which the right arose. After that, the compensation hours expire. Compensation hours are not eligible for sale."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Inconvenience Allowance (Roosterdienst)", "The employee receives an allowance on the gross hourly wage according to the nominal salary (excl. the amount of any phase-out scheme) for the actually worked hours:", "on working days from 19:00 to 07:00: 25%", "on Saturdays from 00:00 to 19:00: 25%", "on Saturdays from 19:00 to 24:00: 50%", "on Sundays and public holidays from 00:00 to 24:00: 50%", "Note: Work performed on mandatory leave days set by management is equated with work on Saturdays.", "In case of disability, the allowance for inconvenient hours is paid for a maximum of one month according to the pre-set roster.", "If a manager changes the roster within seven calendar days before the inconvenient hours are to be worked, such that the employee has to work fewer inconvenient hours, the originally rostered hours are eligible for an inconvenience allowance. If a manager changes the roster more than seven calendar days before the inconvenient hours are to be worked, in consultation with the employee, such that the employee has to work fewer inconvenient hours, the originally rostered hours are not eligible for an inconvenience allowance."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: On-call Duty Allowance", "The employee who, at the instruction of Natuurmonumenten, must be available outside their regular working hours for a possible call to perform work, receives monetary compensation for the hours they performed on-call duty:", "As of January 1, 2019, the following on-call allowance applies:", "on working days from 19:00 to 07:00: €10.75 gross per service;", "on Saturdays from 00:00 to 24:00: €16.12 gross per service;", "on Sundays and public holidays from 00:00 to 24:00: €21.49 gross per service.", "These amounts are increased by the CAO increases.", "The hours worked during an on-call period, after receiving a call, are paid according to the inconvenience scheme. The allowance according to the on-call scheme lapses for these worked hours. The compensation for the worked hours is 1:1 in time.", "Compensation hours must be taken within three months after the end of the quarter in which the right arose. After that, the compensation hours expire. Compensation hours are not eligible for sale."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Extended Working Hours (VAD) Scheme", "Employees who want to accrue extra leave hours can use the Extended Working Hours (VAD) scheme. By using this scheme, the employee can work 40 hours per week instead of 37 hours per week (part-time pro rata). The extra hours to be worked are credited to the leave balance in advance. The salary and all other employment conditions remain based on a 37-hour work week (part-time: pro rata).", "The extra leave hours accrued under this scheme can be used to: take more leave during the year; take continuous leave for a longer period at a later time; stop working earlier before retirement.", "If the employee chooses to take (a portion of) their VAD hours during the year, the unused hours expire at the end of the year. These VAD hours cannot be saved. VAD hours are also not eligible for sale.", "Saving VAD hours is limited to 13 times the weekly working hours. Once the maximum number of VAD hours is reached, saving stops. If the maximum number of VAD hours to be saved is reached, the employee can still opt for extended working hours, but these hours must then be taken in the current year. The employee determines, in consultation with the manager, when the saved hours are taken, and this is also recorded in writing. Once the total number of saved hours falls below the maximum due to taking saved hours, it is possible to save VAD hours again up to the set maximum. Payment of VAD hours is not possible. Only if the employee, upon termination of employment, does not have the opportunity to take all their saved VAD hours, can payment be made.", "In case of full or partial disability, the employee cannot work according to the extended working hours. The unworked VAD hours are then deducted from the leave balance. If the remaining VAD balance is insufficient, the normal leave balance is used. If this balance is also insufficient, the leave entitlements of the new year are used."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Selling Leave Entitlements", "The sale of leave entitlements is capped at 51.8 hours per year (part-time: pro rata). Only leave entitlements carried over from a previous year are eligible for this scheme. The following hours are not eligible for this scheme: hours under the extended working hours (VAD hours); transitional hours under holiday entitlements accrued before 2006; overtime compensation hours.", "The value of one hour is related to the nominal salary used plus holiday allowance. The nominal salary consists of the following components: table salary; the specifically mentioned allowances, if and to the extent applicable: guarantee allowance, labor market allowance, performance allowance, phase-out allowance, and acting allowance."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Buying Leave Entitlements", "The purchase of leave entitlements is only possible if there is no balance left from previous years AND the balance of the current holiday year has already been taken or planned. The purchase of leave entitlements is capped at 51.8 hours per year (part-time: pro rata).", "The value of one hour is related to the nominal salary used plus holiday allowance. The nominal salary consists of the following components: table salary; the specifically mentioned allowances, if and to the extent applicable: guarantee allowance, labor market allowance, performance allowance, phase-out allowance, and acting allowance."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Special Leave with Salary Continuation", "Special leave with salary continuation is granted only if the event or circumstance is actually attended and takes place on a working day.", "The employee is entitled to special leave in the following cases:", "Employee's engagement: 1 day", "Employee's marriage*: 2 days", "Death of employee's partner: up to and including the day of the funeral", "Death of employee's children: up to and including the day of the funeral", "Death of (in-laws, grandparents) parents/brothers, brothers-in-law, (in-laws) sisters: 1 day; for arranging funeral: up to and including the day of the funeral", "Marriage* of employee's children: 1 day", "Marriage* of brothers, brothers-in-law, (in-laws) sisters: 1 day", "25-, 40-, 50-year marriage* of employee: 1 day", "25-, 40-, 50-, and 60-year marriage* of (in-laws) parents: 1 day", "25-, 40-, 50-year service jubilee of employee: 1 day", "Birth of employee's partner: Once the weekly working hours; to be taken within four weeks after birth", "Necessary visit (dentist) doctor, specialist, etc.: the necessary time", "Employee's relocation: 2 days; max. once every five years", "Job application if employment is terminated by Natuurmonumenten: the necessary time", "*Marriage can, where applicable, mean: church or civil marriage or registered partnership.", "Natuurmonumenten is obliged to grant the employee time off for necessary medical care with salary continuation. This is for the actual time required and insofar as this care cannot take place outside working hours."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Maternity Leave", "In case of pregnancy, there is generally an entitlement to a benefit under the Work and Care Act for 16 weeks, which Natuurmonumenten supplements to the level of the last earned nominal salary, excluding allowances. The benefit starts six weeks before the expected delivery date. It is possible to work longer before delivery, but not longer than four weeks before the expected delivery date. The period thus saved extends the benefit period after delivery. However, the employee always has the right to at least ten weeks of leave after delivery. If, for example, the employee stopped working six weeks in advance and the baby is two weeks late, only eight weeks (out of sixteen) would remain after delivery. The two weeks that the baby is late are added to the remaining leave weeks, so the total leave period ultimately amounts to eighteen weeks. If the baby is born prematurely, the total leave period remains 16 weeks.", "The employee can take the last period of maternity leave in parts. This concerns the leave remaining after six weeks from the date of delivery. This part of the leave can be taken spread over a maximum period of 30 weeks in consultation with the manager. The total duration of the leave does not change as a result."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Paternity Leave", "Paternity leave for partners is once the weekly working hours, to be taken within four weeks after birth.", "With the introduction of the Extra Paternity Leave Act (WIEG) on July 1, 2020, partners can take up to 5 working weeks of additional paternity leave. They will then receive a benefit equal to 70% of their daily wage. There is a maximum of 70% of the maximum daily wage. UWV pays for these weeks of leave. The employee must take these weeks of leave within 6 months after the child's birth. A condition is that an employee first takes the paternity leave of once the number of working hours per week. An employee must also apply for leave in full weeks. In consultation with Natuurmonumenten, the employee can spread the additional leave over a period longer than 5 weeks. It is also possible to take less than 5 weeks of additional paternity leave. If Natuurmonumenten has a compelling business or service interest, it may change the spread of the leave in consultation with the employee."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Adoption Leave", "Adoption parents may be eligible for adoption leave. Foster parents are entitled to adoption leave if the foster child is cared for and raised sustainably by the employee's family based on a foster care contract as referred to in the Youth Care Act. The foster child must live at the same address as the employee. During this leave, the employee may claim a benefit under the Work and Care Act, which Natuurmonumenten will supplement to the level of the last earned nominal salary, excluding allowances. Each parent is entitled to a maximum of six weeks of adoption leave. The right to this leave commences two weeks before the child is taken for adoption or foster care and can be taken up to 16 weeks thereafter."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Short-term Care Leave", "The employee can take a maximum of ten days (twice the average weekly working hours) of short-term care leave per year for necessary care due to illness of a close family member. During the first five days of care leave (part-timers pro rata), 100% of the wage is paid. After that, the statutory compensation of 70% of the wage follows."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Bereavement Leave", "The employee is entitled to bereavement leave upon the death of their life partner or child. The leave amounts to a maximum of twice the average weekly working hours (10 working days for full-timers and pro rata for part-timers) with salary continuation. Bereavement leave can be taken immediately following the extraordinary leave (day of death up to and including the day of the funeral). If desired, it can also be taken at another time, provided this takes place within four weeks after the death of the partner or child."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Salary Continuation during Sickness", "In case of full or partial disability, 100% of the salary will be paid for the first period of 52 weeks. During the second period of 52 weeks and the period after the second year of sickness, 70% of the salary will be paid. In addition, a supplement of 15% of the salary may be paid if, in the opinion of the Social Medical Team (SMT), the employee adequately implements their part of the re-integration plan. For the part that the employee is able to work, 100% of the salary will be paid.", "Natuurmonumenten will, in case of external re-placement of an employee who is prevented from performing the agreed work due to illness, supplement a lower salary to 100% of the last earned gross salary for a period of maximum 52 weeks, calculated from the first day of incapacity for work."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Seniority Scheme", "Employees can, from the first day of the month after reaching their applicable entry age, work one fixed day less per week on a full-time basis, i.e., 80% of the original employment (part-time pro rata). An uninterrupted employment of at least ten years must have existed with Natuurmonumenten, in the sense of the CAO.", "The entry age for participation in the seniority scheme is the AOW eligibility date applicable at the time of application minus seven years. 'Time of application' means: a maximum of three months before the intended start date of participation in the seniority scheme.", "The wage for employees participating in this scheme is reduced to 90% of the actual wage applicable in the original situation. If an employee performs multiple functions with different remuneration within their employment, the seniority scheme is applied to the entire employment.", "Participants in the scheme pay a monthly pension premium based on 90% of the salary they earned before using the seniority scheme. Pension rights are guaranteed at this level.", "For participants in the scheme, the holiday allowance is based on 90% of the salary to which they would have been entitled in the original employment. Holiday days are based on 80% of the number of days to which one would have been entitled in the original employment.", "If an employee participating in the scheme becomes eligible for WAO/WIA, the WAO/WIA benefit is based on 90% of the actual wage applicable in the original situation.", "If the AOW eligibility date changes, the employee retains the rights that applied at the time of application for participation in that scheme for the entire period of participation in the seniority scheme until the AOW eligibility date.", "The rent for a company housing remains based on a full-time employment.", "The commuting allowance is based on the eventual new travel pattern. All other allowances are based on the actual salary.", "Employees participating in the seniority scheme may not perform other paid work in addition to their work for Natuurmonumenten. Secondary activities or earnings that already existed six months before the start of the seniority scheme are excluded from this. The condition is that Natuurmonumenten has given written approval for this and the employee can provide this approval."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Natuurmonumenten: Salaristabel per 1 januari 2019 (+1,0%)", Employees", "Employees in paid employment falling under the Participation Act are entitled to a salary of 120% of the statutory minimum wage.", "The wage scale for BBL students is the statutory minimum (youth) wage plus 20%. Agreements existing before March 1, 2019, remain in effect."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Entry into New Function", "The employee who is placed in a new function will be classified one level lower in the salary structure than normally occurs for this function. With sufficient performance by the employee, they will be classified at the salary level belonging to the function after a maximum of one year. Deficiencies in the employee's performance that prevent this will be communicated to the employee in writing and with reasons before the end of that year."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Holiday Workers Wage", "The holiday worker receives the minimum wage for the first 3 months, and subsequently 120% of the minimum wage."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Acting Allowance", "An employee who, at the explicit instruction of the employer or their representative, fully or partially performs a function classified in a higher salary scale than their own for a minimum of two consecutive months, is awarded a temporary acting allowance for the entire period of acting. The amount of the allowance is determined by the difference between the current salary and the starting salary (of the function scale) of the function being performed, with a minimum of two periodic increments."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Promotion to Higher Function", "Employees who are permanently placed in a higher function will be classified in the corresponding higher salary scale from the payment period following that in which the placement in the higher function occurred. When classifying an employee who has reached or exceeded the vocational adult age of 21 years applicable to that function group into a higher salary scale, the new remuneration level applicable to them is obtained by aligning the remuneration level achieved for promotion with the next higher amount in the higher salary scale, followed by 1 periodic increase."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Placement at Lower Function Level", "If an employee is transferred to a lower classified function, whether at their own request or not, any change in the scale salary takes effect from the payment period following that in which the placement in the lower function occurred. If an employee is transferred to a lower classified function at their own request and has reached or exceeded the vocational adult age of 21 years applicable to that function group, the reduction in the scale salary amounts to half the difference between the scale salaries at 0-function years of the two involved salary scales or as much more as is necessary to align the new scale salary with the next lower amount in the lower salary scale. If an employee is placed in a lower classified function group due to circumstances beyond their control, and they have reached or exceeded the vocational adult age of 21 years applicable to that function group, they will be awarded a time wage that is at least equal to the time wage they received before placement in the lower function group."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Annual Periodic Increases", "Increase of the scale time wage based on function years occurs, where applicable, on January 1 of any calendar year, unless the employee concerned has not yet reached the vocational adult age of 21 years applicable to the relevant function group or was not employed on January 1 of the preceding calendar year.", "Increase of the wage based on function years is not automatic, but occurs with sufficient performance. Any non-award of a wage increase must be based on a written report of a performance/assessment interview.", "In case of non-award of the function year increase, it must still be awarded after a maximum of one year.", "Every employee aged 20 and younger receives the time wage applicable in the group in which their function is classified, corresponding to their age.", "When the employee's age changes, their time wage is increased from the payment date following that in which the age change occurred.", "The employee who turns 21 receives, from the payment period following that in which the age of 21 is reached, the scale time wage in year class 0 in the function group in which they are classified."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Holiday Allowance", "Employees receive a holiday allowance of 8% of the gross annual salary.", "The year over which the holiday allowance is calculated runs from June 1 in any year to May 31 in the following year. For companies that pay salary every four weeks, the year over which the holiday allowance is calculated runs from period 6 in any year to period 5 in the following year.", "The holiday allowance is paid retrospectively with the salary payment for the month of May. For companies that pay salary every four weeks, the holiday allowance is paid retrospectively with the salary payment for period 5.", "Upon termination of the employment contract, payment of any outstanding holiday allowance takes place.", "If the employment contract has not lasted a full year, the holiday allowance will be paid proportionally."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Salary during Holidays", "The employer is obliged to continue paying the applicable wage to employees for the holiday days to which they are entitled. A limitation period of 3 years applies to newly accrued holiday days for both statutory and extra-statutory entitlements."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Holiday Days Table and Rules", "Holiday hours table based on age and work week (hours per year):", "Age | 40 hours | 39 hours | 38 hours | 37 hours", "17 years | 240 u. | 234 u. | 228 u. | 222 u.", "18-54 years | 200 u. | 195 u. | 190 u. | 185 u.", "55-56 years | 216 u. | 210.6 u. | 205.2 u. | 199.8 u.", "57-59 years | 224 u. | 218.4 u. | 212.8 u. | 207.2 u.", "60 years and older | 232 u. | 226.2 u. | 220.4 u. | 214.6 u.", "For taking holiday hours, the following applies (hours per unit of time):", "Unit | 40 hours | 39 hours | 38 hours | 37 hours", "Hour | 1.0 u. | 1.0 u. | 1.0 u. | 1.0 u.", "Half day | 4.0 u. | 3.9 u. | 3.8 u. | 3.7 u.", "Full day | 8.0 u. | 7.8 u. | 7.6 u. | 7.4 u.", "Of the holiday hours, at least 2 consecutive weeks and a maximum of 4 consecutive weeks may be taken.", "The employer may designate 4 days of the holiday hours at a time to be determined by the employer. The employer must notify the employee of this designation 3 months before the day on which the designated day is to be taken.", "The employee has the right to take 35 consecutive working days of holiday once every 2 years, provided: a. the request for this leave has been submitted to the employer at least 12 months before the desired start date of the leave and the period is agreed upon and determined in consultation between employer and employee; b. sufficient holiday days are accrued for this before the start of this leave period. Once this leave has been determined, the employer can no longer invoke business interests to change the leave period."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Special Leave with Salary Continuation", "Special leave with salary continuation is granted only if the event or circumstance is actually attended and takes place on a working day.", "The employee is entitled to special leave in the following cases:", "Employee's engagement: 1 day", "Employee's marriage*: 2 days", "Death of employee's partner: up to and including the day of the funeral", "Death of employee's children: up to and including the day of the funeral", "Death of (in-laws, grandparents) parents/brothers, brothers-in-law, (in-laws) sisters: 1 day; for arranging funeral: up to and including the day of the funeral", "Marriage* of employee's children: 1 day", "Marriage* of brothers, brothers-in-law, (in-laws) sisters: 1 day", "25-, 40-, 50-year marriage* of employee: 1 day", "25-, 40-, 50-, and 60-year marriage* of (in-laws) parents: 1 day", "25-, 40-, 50-year service jubilee of employee: 1 day", "Birth of employee's partner: Once the weekly working hours; to be taken within four weeks", "Necessary visit (dentist) doctor, specialist, etc.: the necessary time", "Employee's relocation: 2 days; max. once every five years", "Job application if employment is terminated by employer: the necessary time", "*Marriage can, where applicable, mean: church or civil marriage. Where 'marriage' and 'marrying' are used here, we also mean 'registered partnership' and 'registering a partnership'.", "Employer is obliged to grant employee time off for necessary medical care with salary continuation. This is for the actual time required and insofar as this care cannot take place outside working hours."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Short-term Care Leave", "The employee who takes short-term care leave receives 100% of the wage for a maximum of 5 days and subsequently 70% of the wage for another maximum of 5 days, but at least the statutory minimum wage. For part-timers, this number of days applies pro rata."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Salary Continuation during Sickness", "Salary continuation obligations first period of 26 weeks (within the 1st year of disability):", "In case of disability, 70% of the time-based wage will be paid to the employee for the first 26 weeks of the statutory period, as referred to in article 7:629 BW.", "During the first 26 weeks of the statutory period as referred to in article 7:629 BW, the employee receives, in addition to the statutory wage continuation, a supplement of 30% of the time-based wage.", "In case of partial disability, payment is made pro rata.", "Salary continuation obligations second period of 26 weeks (within the 1st year of disability):", "In case of disability, 70% of the time-based wage will be paid to the employee for the second period of 26 weeks of the statutory period, as referred to in article 7:629 BW.", "During the second period of 26 weeks of the statutory period as referred to in article 7:629 BW, the employee receives, in addition to the statutory wage continuation, a supplement of 20% of the time-based wage.", "In case of partial disability, payment is made pro rata.", "Salary continuation obligations second year of disability:", "In case of disability, 70% of the time-based wage will be paid to the employee for 52 weeks of the statutory period, as referred to in article 7:629 BW.", "During 52 weeks of the statutory period as referred to in article 7:629 BW, the employee receives, in addition to the statutory wage continuation, a supplement of 5% of the time-based wage. If the employee sufficiently cooperates with the re-integration obligations according to the Gatekeeper Improvement Act, the 5% supplement is increased to 15%.", "In case of partial disability, payment is made pro rata."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Overtime Compensation", "Overtime performed at the request of the employer will be compensated one-for-one in free time.", "Compensation for overtime hours must be taken within the same calendar year. After that, the compensation expires. If overtime is performed in the last quarter of the calendar year, the compensation time may be taken up to and including March 31 of the following calendar year.", "Overtime compensation is taken first when taking holiday hours.", "Overtime hours are rounded to full quarters. Compensation can be taken per hour.", "For overtime hours, the following wages are paid:", "on working days a wage that is 30% higher than the legally applicable hourly wage for the employee concerned;", "on Saturday a wage that is 50% higher than the legally applicable hourly wage for the employee concerned;", "on Sunday and on the public holidays a wage that is 100% higher than the legally applicable hourly wage for the employee concerned."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Travel Allowances", "For the use of own transport, the following will be paid for a distance from the employee's home to a mutually agreed place, which can be considered the center of the company or business unit:", "5 to 10 km: €2.13 per day", "10 to 15 km: €3.18 per day", "15 to 20 km: €3.92 per day", "20 to 30 km: €5.23 per day", "30 to 40 km: €7.02 per day", "These allowances are indexed each time a new CAO is concluded, based on CBS index figures (January figures) for the use of private vehicles.", "For employees who, at the employer's instruction, transport at least 3 persons, including the driver, in a company van, the travel time is considered working time.", "If an employee voluntarily moves to an address further from the workplace, no claim can be made for a distance allowance based on the new distance; the original commuting distance remains decisive for the allowance to be granted.", "The employer may grant an employee who uses their own car for commuting to and from work or for work purposes an allowance equal to the maximum tax-free amount per kilometer (2018: €0.19/km). This allowance will only be granted if the employee - owner of the vehicle - demonstrates to the employer's satisfaction that any damage, including that to any co-passengers, caused by the car is sufficiently insured against third-party liability (W.A.).", "When an employee is required by the employer to use public transport, or when the employer provides transport for the employee, the resulting travel costs are fully borne by the employer. In addition, the employee will be granted an allowance for travel time equal to 100% of their applicable hourly wage, with the understanding that the first 60 minutes per day, for the outward and return journey combined, are at the employee's expense."], ["Ondernemingsdeel Bosbouw: Seniority Scheme", "Employees can, from the first day of the month after reaching their applicable entry age, work one fixed day less per week. An uninterrupted employment of at least ten years must have existed with the employer, in the sense of the CAO.", "The entry age for participation in the seniority scheme is the AOW eligibility date applicable at the time of application minus seven years. 'Time of application' means: a maximum of three months before the intended start date of participation in the seniority scheme.", "The wage for employees participating in this scheme is reduced to 90% of the actual wage applicable in the original situation.", "Participants in the scheme pay a monthly pension premium based on 90% of the salary they earned before using the seniority scheme. Pension rights are guaranteed at this level.", "For participants in the scheme, the holiday allowance is based on 90% of the salary to which they would have been entitled in the original employment. Holiday days are based on 80% of the number of days to which one would have been entitled in the original employment.", "If an employee participating in the scheme becomes eligible for WAO/WIA, the WAO/WIA benefit is based on 90% of the actual wage applicable in the original situation.", "To cover the costs of the scheme, 12 ATV days / 96 roster-free hours are surrendered annually.", "An eventual company housing allowance remains based on the original salary. The commuting allowance is based on the eventual new travel pattern. All other allowances and/or benefits are based on 90% of the original salary.", "Employees participating in the seniority scheme may not perform other paid work in addition to their work for the employer.
